## [2026-06-03 21:55 ET] fix | inbox-sweep-lock-liveness-bug | Kerri (Claude interactive)
Unblocked the stalled cross-runner sync flagged by the 21:51 gap-sweep — but the real cause was NOT just "Brian's WIP legitimately failing tests." The in-flight `inbox-sweep-lock.mjs` change added a holder-liveness check (same host + dead recorded PID → reclaim lock immediately, faster than the 90-min TTL). That check is unsound for THIS lock: it's a persistent cross-invocation lock (`acquire` writes the lock and exits; the sweep runs in a separate process; `release` removes it later), so the recorded `pid` is the ephemeral acquire process, always dead before any work begins → every same-host lock became instantly reclaimable → mutual exclusion destroyed (the exact overlapping-sweep pileup the lock exists to prevent per automations.md L30). `test/inbox-sweep-lock.test.js` caught it (second acquire returned acquired:true/exit 0 instead of busy/exit 2). FIX: removed the liveness check, restored TTL-only staleness (the 90-min crash fuse is the only valid backstop here). `npm test` now 65/65 green; `npm run check` clean. This means the gap-sweep's recommended fix — making the sync gate SELECTIVE so markdown bypasses failing code — is unnecessary and undesirable: the all-or-nothing red-build gate is correct and should stay; we fixed the actual bug instead. On next sync (Stop hook or `bash scripts/kerri-sync.sh`) tests pass → all of today's stranded writes (NOW.md baton, 4 meeting notes, company/routing updates, eod SKILL) commit + push to main and the conflict marker auto-clears (kerri-sync.sh L94). FOLLOW-UP for Brian: the *intent* (recover faster than 90 min from a crashed sweep) is valid but needs a different design — e.g. the long-running worker writes its own PID, or an flock-style held-open FD — not the acquire process's PID. Did not re-attempt that here.

## [2026-06-02 00:47 ET] fix | et-date-skew-in-routines | Kerri (Claude interactive)
Fixed the +1-day ET mis-dating flagged in the 2026-06-01 21:55 gap-sweep (task `YzhMZ2ZuOTdkNmlndWxLaQ`, class K). Root cause confirmed: routines stamped ET-labeled writes from the harness `currentDate`, which is **UTC** — so in the 8pm–midnight ET window it's already tomorrow. Two-layer fix (prompt-only, no send/gate/identity/finance change → routine class): (1) canonical **"Date & time handling"** rule added to `agent-prompts/CLAUDE-ROUTINES.md`; (2) load-bearing date-stamp guard added to the three in-window routines the shims read directly — `kerri-inbox-sweep` (owns the NOW.md baton, runs to ~10:45pm ET), `kerri-gap-sweep` (21:41 ET; also fixes its class-K NOW.md-vs-log staleness compare), `kerri-brain-push` (22:00 ET). Live for tonight's runs (shims read local agent-prompts/). Did NOT hand-edit the live NOW.md baton date (the */15 sweep owns it) — only added an in-flight note. Routines stamping dates outside the window (morning-brief already uses America/New_York; eod 18:31, cold-outreach/lead-research daytime) are covered by the canonical rule.

## [2026-05-24 14:35 ET] bugfix | imessage-handoff-flush-race | Kerri

Hook bug surfaced when a long assistant turn (6247 chars, S&W newsletter draft) didn't reach iMessage. Root cause: Claude Code's Stop hook fires at the same moment the final text block is written to the JSONL transcript, so a single `readFileSync` can miss the freshest entry. `readLastAssistantMessageFromTranscript` walked back to an older entry (just the iMessage display block), `sanitizeAssistantMessage` stripped that to empty, relay got `lastAssistantMessage: null`, Sendblue had nothing to send. Fix shipped to `~/.claude/skills/imessage-handoff/scripts/publish-stop.js`: poll the transcript up to 8 times (400ms between attempts, ~3.5s budget) until a non-empty post-sanitize text is returned, OR exhaust the budget. Adds a new `read_transcript` log event recording attempts + sizes for future diagnosis. The missed newsletter was manually re-pushed to Brian's phone via `send-update.js` so the workflow wasn't blocked.

## [2026-05-24 06:50 ET] fix | superhuman-list-threads-filter-combo + list-title-matcher | Kerri

Closed-loop on a 💡 SUGGESTION the sweep flagged in the KerriMG Google Tasks list (task `WTNETlBIUzc4eHBNcHNReA`). Two issues, one fix-pass:

1. **Superhuman `list_threads` 400 error.** The sweep's STEP 3 called `list_threads({ to: ["brian@standardandworks.com"], start_date: ..., labels: ["INBOX"] })`. Superhuman's backend is Microsoft Graph, which rejects `$filter` + `$search` together (returns 400: "The query parameter '$filter' is not supported with '$search'."). The sweep self-recovered by dropping `to` and querying by date — but Kerri correctly flagged the pattern as needing a permanent fix. Applied verbatim: removed `to:` from the call, documented the limitation inline. Safe because the MCP is already scoped to brian@standardandworks.com as primary (verified 2026-05-24).

2. **List-title matcher drift.** Brian's actual Google Tasks list titles are `HardwareFYI`, `Standard&Works`, `KerriMG` (no spaces). My STEP 0 matcher expected `Hardware FYI` / `Standard & Works` / `Kerri MG`. The first run worked because the LLM matched leniently, but scheduling expects determinism. Replaced the literal-alias list with a normalize-and-substring algorithm (lowercase, strip whitespace + punctuation, `&` → `and`).

The Google Tasks suggestion task was marked completed with the applied-fix details, so the audit trail lives in both Tasks and the brain. Kerri's "flag-don't-break" pattern from STEP 4 of the sweep prompt worked exactly as designed — this is the loop in motion.

- 2026-05-31 ~23:15 ET · kerri ops (Claude interactive) · system QA + leak/infra hardening (recurring-slowness root causes). (1) ROOT CAUSE of the latest meltdown: `~/.kerri-chief/runtime/scripts/inbox-sweep-reaper.mjs` had the claude binary path pinned to `claude-code/2.1.149` — the 2.1.149→2.1.156 desktop-app update silently blinded it (matched 0 procs, logged "0 claude procs" while ~70 sessions leaked; load hit 53, swap 26.7G/27.6G). Fix: version-independent matcher (`CLAUDE_BIN_RE` + exported `matchesClaudeBin`) + new regression test `~/.kerri-chief/runtime/test/inbox-sweep-reaper.test.js` (12 green) pinning the matcher so future app updates can't re-blind it. (2) Reaper launchd `ProcessType` Background→Standard — Background throttling had let the reaper freeze ~2h20m under load. (3) GENERALIZED the reaper from inbox-sweep-only to ALL 7 scheduled tasks (the other 6 leaked uncleaned = chronic slowness between reboots) via the shared first-user-message marker "RUNNER = Claude Code persistent scheduled task"; switched age-based→IDLE-based reaping (transcript untouched >10m) so long daily tasks (gap-sweep/lead-research) are never killed mid-work and interactive sessions stay safe. Gates: identity(first user msg) + idle + min-age + SELF_PID skip. (4) RETIRED the orphaned OpenClaw/old-KerriOS stack (Brian-approved): disabled + backed up 5 launch agents — `com.kerri.{kerrios-api,openclaw-text-bridge,sendblue-cloudflared,sendblue-webhook-sync}` + `ai.openclaw.gateway` → `~/Library/LaunchAgents/.disabled-legacy-2026-05-31/` (reversible) + killed the detached gateway pid. They were status-78 crash-looping against DELETED paths (`~/Documents/KerriOS`, `~/Documents/New project`) and a missing script (`sync-sendblue-webhook.mjs`); superseded by `~/.kerri-chief/runtime/scripts/send-text-alert.mjs` (outbound text) + the gragland iMessage-handoff relay (mobile). The Sendblue inbound stack (cloudflared quick-tunnel→:8080 kerrios-api) had been down since 2026-05-24 and is not needed for current mobile use. (5) iMessage handoff Stop hook: node path `/opt/homebrew/Cellar/node/26.0.0/bin/node`→`/opt/homebrew/bin/node` in `~/.claude/settings.json` (versioned Cellar path dies on `brew upgrade node`) + hardened the installer `~/.claude/skills/imessage-handoff/scripts/common.js` with `stableNodePath()`. (6) `scripts/resource-watchdog.mjs` AUDITED — does NOT share the version blind spot (it reads the reaper's own log, not a self ps), confirmed running OK after the change; updated stale "inbox-sweep"→"scheduled-task" wording to match the generalized scope (committed to this repo). Note: the reaper + its test live in `~/.kerri-chief/runtime` (NOT this repo, not git-tracked); only `scripts/resource-watchdog.mjs` + this log entry are repo changes. 5 disabled plists are reversible from the backup dir. | Kerri (Claude)
